Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The asteroids operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 477. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 48 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to asteroids theory, we decompose the problem into 477 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 48. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
